Currently registers email + webhook handlers only.\n7. **`internal/queue/server.go`** — `Server.RegisterHandler(taskType, handler)` and `Server.Start()`, `Server.Stop()`\n8. **`internal/model/chunk.go`** — `Chunk{ID, OrgID, KnowledgeBaseID, DocumentID, SourceID, Content, ChunkIndex, TokenCount, PageNumber, Heading, ChunkType, Metadata}`\n\n## What To Create\n\n### 1. `internal/jobs/document_process.go`\n\nA handler function matching `func(ctx context.Context, task *asynq.Task) error`:\n\n```go\nfunc NewDocumentProcessHandler(\n    pool *pgxpool.Pool,\n    docRepo *repository.DocumentRepository,\n    chunkRepo *repository.ChunkRepository, \n    store storage.Client,\n) asynq.HandlerFunc\n```\n\nThe handler should:\n1. Unmarshal `DocumentProcessPayload` from `task.Payload()`\n2. Fetch document record from DB to get `storage_path` (the SeaweedFS fid)\n3. Download file content from SeaweedFS via `store.Download(ctx, fid)`\n4. Read the content into a string\n5. Split into chunks by markdown headings (`## ` boundaries). Each section becomes a chunk with:\n   - `Heading` = the heading text (e.g. \"Overview\", \"Details\", \"Reviews\")\n   - `Content` = the section content (including the heading)\n   - `ChunkIndex` = sequential index\n   - `ChunkType` = \"text\"\n   - `TokenCount` = approximate: `len(strings.Fields(content))`\n   - `OrgID`, `KnowledgeBaseID`, `DocumentID` from the payload\n6. Insert each chunk via `chunkRepo.CreateChunk(ctx, tx, chunk)` inside a `db.WithOrgID` transaction\n7. Update document status to \"ready\" via direct SQL or document repo\n\nFor the markdown splitting, use a simple approach:\n- Split on lines starting with `## ` (keep `# ` title as part of first chunk)\n- Each section = heading + content until next heading\n\n### 2. 1	// Package main is the entry point for the Asynq background worker process.
```
2	// It connects to Valkey using the same config as the API server and processes
3	// async tasks (document processing, URL scraping, KB reindexing, webhook delivery).
4	package main
5	
6	import (
7		"context"
8		"log"
9		"log/slog"
10		"os"
11		"os/signal"

14		"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/config"

17		"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/queue"
18		"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/repository"
19		"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/storage"
20	)

22	func main() {
23		cfg, err := config.Load()
24		if err != nil {
25			log.Fatalf("failed to load config: %v", err)
26		}
27	
28		logger := slog.Default()
29	
30		// Connect to the database so job handlers can access it.
31		pool, err := db.New(context.Background(), cfg.Database.URL)
32		if err != nil {
33			log.Fatalf("failed to connect to database: %v", err)
34		}
35		defer pool.Close()
36	
37		notifRepo := repository.NewNotificationRepository(pool)
38		docRepo := repository.NewDocumentRepository(pool)
39		chunkRepo := repository.NewChunkRepository(pool)
40		storageClient := storage.NewSeaweedFSClient(cfg.SeaweedFS.MasterURL, nil)
41	
42		srv := queue.NewServer(queue.ServerConfig{
43			RedisAddr:   cfg.Valkey.URL,
44			Concurrency: cfg.Queue.Concurrency,
45			MaxRetry:    cfg.Queue.MaxRetry,
46			Logger:      logger,
47		})
48	
49		// Register email delivery handler.
50		srv.Mux().HandleFunc(queue.TypeSendEmail, jobs.HandleSendEmail(notifRepo))
51	
52		// Register the webhook delivery handler on the server mux.
53		webhookRepo := repository.NewWebhookRepository(pool)
54		webhookDeliveryHandler := jobs.NewWebhookDeliveryHandler(pool, webhookRepo, logger)
55		srv.Mux().Handle(queue.TypeWebhookDelivery, webhookDeliveryHandler)
56	
57		// Register document processing handler (overrides the stub in queue.Server).
58		srv.Mux().HandleFunc(queue.TypeDocumentProcess,
59			jobs.NewDocumentProcessHandler(pool, docRepo, chunkRepo, storageClient, logger))
60	
61		errCh := make(chan error, 1)
62	
63		// Start worker in a goroutine so we can listen for shutdown signals.
64		go func() {
65			if err := srv.Start(); err != nil {
66				errCh <- err
67			}
68		}()
69	
70		// Wait for interrupt signal or server error.
71		sigCh := make(chan os.Signal, 1)
72		signal.Notify(sigCh, syscall.SIGINT, syscall.SIGTERM)
73		select {
74		case sig := <-sigCh:
75			logger.Info("received signal, shutting down worker", "signal", sig)
76		case err := <-errCh:
77			logger.Error("asynq server error, shutting down", "error", err)
78		}
79	
80		srv.Shutdown()
81		pool.Close()
82		logger.Info("worker exited gracefully")
83	}
84
```

```
// Package main is the entry point for the Asynq background worker process.
// It connects to Valkey using the same config as the API server and processes
// async tasks (document processing, URL scraping, KB reindexing, webhook delivery).
package main

import (
	"context"
	"log"
	"log/slog"
	"os"
	"os/signal"
	"syscall"

	"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/config"
	"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/db"
	"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/jobs"
	"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/queue"
	"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/repository"
	"github.com/ravencloak-org/Raven/internal/storage"
)

func main() {
	cfg, err := config.Load()
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("failed to load config: %v", err)
	}

	logger := slog.Default()

	// Connect to the database so job handlers can access it.
	pool, err := db.New(context.Background(), cfg.Database.URL)
	if err != nil {
		log.Fatalf("failed to connect to database: %v", err)
	}
	defer pool.Close()

	notifRepo := repository.NewNotificationRepository(pool)
	docRepo := repository.NewDocumentRepository(pool)
	chunkRepo := repository.NewChunkRepository(pool)
	storageClient := storage.NewSeaweedFSClient(cfg.SeaweedFS.MasterURL, nil)

	srv := queue.NewServer(queue.ServerConfig{
		RedisAddr:   cfg.Valkey.URL,
		Concurrency: cfg.Queue.Concurrency,
		MaxRetry:    cfg.Queue.MaxRetry,
		Logger:      logger,
	})

	// Register email delivery handler.
	srv.Mux().HandleFunc(queue.TypeSendEmail, jobs.HandleSendEmail(notifRepo))

	// Register the webhook delivery handler on the server mux.
	webhookRepo := repository.NewWebhookRepository(pool)
	webhookDeliveryHandler := jobs.NewWebhookDeliveryHandler(pool, webhookRepo, logger)
	srv.Mux().Handle(queue.TypeWebhookDelivery, webhookDeliveryHandler)

	// Register document processing handler (overrides the stub in queue.Server).
	srv.Mux().HandleFunc(queue.TypeDocumentProcess,
		jobs.NewDocumentProcessHandler(pool, docRepo, chunkRepo, storageClient, logger))
```

```
127			// Mark as chunking.
128			if err := updateDocStatus(ctx, pool, p.OrgID, p.DocumentID, docRepo, model.ProcessingStatusChunking, ""); err != nil {
129				return fmt.Errorf("set status chunking: %w", err)
130			}
131	
132			// Split markdown into sections by heading.
133			sections := SplitMarkdownByHeadings(content)
134			if len(sections) == 0 {
135				// No content to chunk — mark as ready with zero chunks.
136				if err := updateDocStatus(ctx, pool, p.OrgID, p.DocumentID, docRepo, model.ProcessingStatusReady, ""); err != nil {
137					return fmt.Errorf("set status ready (empty): %w", err)
138				}
139				return nil
140			}
141	
142			// Insert all chunks and update status in a single RLS transaction.
143			err = db.WithOrgID(ctx, pool, p.OrgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
144				for i, section := range sections {
145					if strings.TrimSpace(section.Content) == "" {
146						continue
147					}
148					tokenCount := len(strings.Fields(section.Content))
149					chunk := &model.Chunk{
150						OrgID:           p.OrgID,
151						KnowledgeBaseID: p.KnowledgeBaseID,
152						DocumentID:      &p.DocumentID,
153						Content:         section.Content,
154						ChunkIndex:      i,
155						TokenCount:      &tokenCount,
156						ChunkType:       model.ChunkTypeText,
157						Metadata:        map[string]any{},
158					}
159					if section.Heading != "" {
160						chunk.Heading = &section.Heading
161					}
162					if _, err := chunkRepo.CreateChunk(ctx, tx, chunk); err != nil {
163						return fmt.Errorf("create chunk %d: %w", i, err)
164					}
165				}
166	
167				// Update document status to ready within the same transaction.
168				return docRepo.UpdateStatus(ctx, tx, p.OrgID, p.DocumentID, model.ProcessingStatusReady, "")
169			})
170			if err != nil {
171				setFailed(ctx, pool, p.OrgID, p.DocumentID, docRepo, err)
172				return fmt.Errorf("insert chunks: %w", err)
173			}
174	
175			logger.Info("document processed",
176				"document_id", p.DocumentID,
177				"chunks", len(sections),
178			)
179	
180			return nil
181		}
182	}

184	// updateDocStatus is a convenience wrapper that updates document processing status
```

```
186	func updateDocStatus(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool, orgID, docID string, docRepo *repository.DocumentRepository, status model.ProcessingStatus, errMsg string) error {
187		return db.WithOrgID(ctx, pool, orgID, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
188			return docRepo.UpdateStatus(ctx, tx, orgID, docID, status, errMsg)
189		})
190	}

192	// setFailed marks a document as failed, logging any secondary error.
193	func setFailed(ctx context.Context, pool *pgxpool.Pool, orgID, docID string, docRepo *repository.DocumentRepository, cause error) {
194		if err := updateDocStatus(ctx, pool, orgID, docID, docRepo, model.ProcessingStatusFailed, cause.Error()); err != nil {
195			slog.Default().Warn("failed to mark document as failed",
196				"document_id", docID,
```

```
1	package auth

3	import (
4		"github.com/supertokens/supertokens-golang/recipe/session"
5		"github.com/supertokens/supertokens-golang/recipe/session/sessmodels"
6		"github.com/supertokens/supertokens-golang/recipe/thirdparty"
7		"github.com/supertokens/supertokens-golang/recipe/thirdparty/tpmodels"
8		"github.com/supertokens/supertokens-golang/supertokens"
9	)
10	
11	// SuperTokensInitConfig holds the parameters required to initialise the
12	// SuperTokens Go SDK. APIDomain and WebsiteDomain come from config so that
13	// the same binary can run locally (localhost) or in production (ravencloak.org).
14	type SuperTokensInitConfig struct {
15		ConnectionURI string
16		APIKey        string
17		APIDomain     string // e.g. https://api.ravencloak.org
18		WebsiteDomain string // e.g. https://app.ravencloak.org
19	}
20	
21	// InitSuperTokens initialises the SuperTokens Go SDK with the ThirdParty and
22	// Session recipes. The SDK must be initialised once, before any route
23	// registration, so that supertokens.Middleware can intercept /auth/* paths.
24	func InitSuperTokens(cfg SuperTokensInitConfig) error {
25		apiBasePath := "/auth"
26		websiteBasePath := "/auth"
27	
28		// Cookie domain for cross-subdomain session sharing.
29		// api.ravencloak.org sets cookies, app.ravencloak.org reads them.
30		cookieDomain := ".ravencloak.org"
31		if cfg.APIDomain == "" || cfg.APIDomain == "http://localhost:8081" {
32			cookieDomain = "localhost"
33		}
34	
35		recipeList := []supertokens.Recipe{
36			thirdparty.Init(&tpmodels.TypeInput{
37				// Provider list is managed via the SuperTokens Core multitenancy API.
38			}),
39			session.Init(&sessmodels.TypeInput{
40				CookieDomain:  &cookieDomain,
41				CookieSameSite: strPtr("none"),
42			}),
43		}
44	
45		return supertokens.Init(supertokens.TypeInput{
46			Supertokens: &supertokens.ConnectionInfo{
47				ConnectionURI: cfg.ConnectionURI,
48				APIKey:        cfg.APIKey,
49			},
50			AppInfo: supertokens.AppInfo{
51				AppName:         "Raven",
52				APIDomain:       cfg.APIDomain,
53				WebsiteDomain:   cfg.WebsiteDomain,
54				APIBasePath:     &apiBasePath,
55				WebsiteBasePath: &websiteBasePath,
56			},
57			RecipeList: recipeList,
58		})
59	}
60	
61	func strPtr(s string) *string { return &s }
62
```

```
1	import { defineStore } from 'pinia'
2	import { ref, computed } from 'vue'
3	import Session from 'supertokens-web-js/recipe/session'
4	import {
5	  getAuthorisationURLWithQueryParamsAndSetState,
6	  signInAndUp,
7	} from 'supertokens-web-js/recipe/thirdparty'
8	import { usePostHog } from '../plugins/posthog'
9	
10	export const useAuthStore = defineStore('auth', () => {
11	  const sessionExists = ref(false)
12	  const orgId = ref<string | null>(sessionStorage.getItem('raven_org_id'))
13	  const isAuthenticated = computed(() => sessionExists.value)
14	  const hasOrg = computed(() => !!orgId.value)
15	
16	  async function init() {
17	    sessionExists.value = await Session.doesSessionExist()
18	  }
19	
20	  async function loginWithGoogle() {
21	    const authUrl = await getAuthorisationURLWithQueryParamsAndSetState({
22	      thirdPartyId: 'google',
23	      frontendRedirectURI: `${window.location.origin}/callback`,
24	    })
25	    window.location.assign(authUrl)
26	  }
27	
28	  async function handleCallback(): Promise<{ isNewUser: boolean; orgId?: string }> {
29	    const response = await signInAndUp()
30	    if (response.status !== 'OK') {
31	      throw new Error('Sign-in failed: ' + response.status)
32	    }
33	    sessionExists.value = true
34	
35	    // Call backend to create/find internal user.
36	    // Uses GET so cookies are sent with SameSite=Lax (POST blocks cross-origin cookies).
37	    const res = await fetch(
38	      `${import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL}/auth/callback`,
39	      { method: 'GET', credentials: 'include' },
40	    )
41	    if (!res.ok) {
42	      throw new Error(`Auth callback failed (${res.status})`)
43	    }
44	    return res.json()
45	  }
46	
47	  async function logout() {
48	    const { reset: resetPostHog } = usePostHog()
49	    resetPostHog()
50	    sessionExists.value = false
51	    orgId.value = null
52	    sessionStorage.removeItem('raven_org_id')
53	    await Session.signOut()
54	    window.location.href = '/login'
55	  }
56	
57	  function setOrgId(id: string) {
58	    orgId.value = id
59	    sessionStorage.setItem('raven_org_id', id)
60	  }
61	
62	  return {
63	    sessionExists,
64	    orgId,
65	    isAuthenticated,
66	    hasOrg,
67	    init,
68	    loginWithGoogle,
69	    handleCallback,
70	    logout,
71	    setOrgId,
72	  }
73	})
74
```

> AGENT

SuperTokens returns tokens in **headers** (`St-Access-Token`, `St-Refresh-Token`), not cookies. This is because cookie domain is `.ravencloak.org` which doesn't match `localhost`. The frontend SDK handles this automatically (header-based auth mode), but the auth callback needs the access token in the `Authorization` header.

The real issue: the frontend's `handleCallback` after signup should work automatically because `supertokens-web-js` intercepts fetch and adds the token headers. The curl test fails because curl doesn't do that.

> AGENT

The issue is that `loginWithEmailPassword` and `signUpWithEmailPassword` call `fetch()` directly for the auth callback — but `supertokens-web-js` only intercepts `fetch` if you import its custom fetch wrapper. The raw `fetch()` won't include the `Authorization: Bearer <st-access-token>` header.

The fix: the SuperTokens web SDK should auto-attach headers to `fetch` calls. But that requires the `Session.init()` to be configured properly. Let me check if `supertokens-web-js` automatically adds an interceptor.

Actually, looking at the SuperTokens docs — `supertokens-web-js` **does** add a global fetch interceptor when `Session.init()` is called. But the domain of the callback URL must match the `apiDomain`. Let me verify the callback URL matches.

The callback uses `VITE_API_BASE_URL` which we just set to `http://localhost:8081/api/v1`. The `apiDomain` is `http://localhost:8081`. These match, so the interceptor should work.

The most likely issue: the user was getting "Failed to fetch" before the `.env.local` fix (when frontend pointed to port 8080 but SuperTokens pointed to 8081). With the fix in place, it should work now. Let me verify the frontend is running with the updated env.
